# Agents · H · part 1

882 entries, h1-research-worker to heiwa-builder. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **h1-research-worker**: Research HackerOne program — scope, bounties, disclosed reports ([overtimepog/greyhatcc](https://github.com/overtimepog/greyhatcc/blob/HEAD/agents/h1-research-worker.md))
- **h22-adjudicator**: Runs the blueprint §10 intake protocol on external research artifacts (dossiers, whitepapers, threads, release notes). Produces a one-page adjudication appendix under docs/intake/ — tier-labeled claims, P1–P7 verdicts, harvest into existing gaps. Never revises the blueprint itself. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-adjudicator.md) · ★10)
- **h22-docsurgeon**: G7 public-claim-surface surgeon. Fixes doc drift on README.md and docs/ only — auth instructions, About/framing language, badge/version reconciliation, loopback-security sentence, C3 Moneta-naming consistency (one-Moneta ruling). Never touches code, tests, or state files. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-docsurgeon.md) · ★10)
- **h22-forge**: Implementation agent for gated H22 blueprint work — G1 port waves, G5 grounding tools, G9 gate build, baseline/freeze code. Refuses any dispatch that lacks a GATEWARDEN ALLOW. Works in worktrees, one atomic commit per wave, never merges or pushes. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-forge.md) · ★10)
- **h22-gatewarden**: Read-only gate-state oracle for the H22 blueprint harness. Reads drop.json, flywheel_queue.json, posture.json and the arming markers, then emits an ALLOW/REFUSE verdict per work item. Never flips a gate; never writes anything. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-gatewarden.md) · ★10)
- **h22-scribe**: Phase-0 paper author for the H22 blueprint. Drafts the Leg-0 specs (port-wave manifest, pre-flight gate, grounding contract, benchmark design) and freezes baselines. Writes ONLY under docs/ and harness/notes/. Every cited path, count, or symbol is verified by its own Read/Grep first. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-scribe.md) · ★10)
